# Release Note

## Important Changes

### Core Cryptographic and Protocol Library
- Adjust DRBG initialization: public/private DRBG no longer use derivation function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/505d44c623c2a883cf015f26a499842cea0161f0)
- Adjust DANE certificate priority: TLSA issuer certificates take precedence over peer certificates in verification. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/661de442e4231a9b0411dc8562f9e465d1d7fabc)
- Fix double-free in `EVP_PKEY_CTX_dup()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/85407b77543a2d4330dbb40f6b8520ea0894a716)
- Fix DH `dupctx` reference count. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21a0d9f3edda78d27d12cd7704de9e32976393ba)
- DECODER now validates that the first decoded structure name matches the caller-specified structure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/73dd5d67c506cfeb9bf6183f0c19832c7d3f174d)
- Change: `PEM to DER decoder: Specify object type and data structure more consistently`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98408852c167d895a662dcda824fd5170cad3f7d)
- Change: `OSSL_STORE 'file:' scheme: Set input structure for certificates and CRLs`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/821b3956ec698927281a5b29c55cd87eb7b2793d)
- Fix boundary check in `BIO_write`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a04b06573e2b3c6a5c703a60bd95354c6c6e91dc)
- Change: `Socket now displays what address it is connecting to`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/994fa5f9861df94c07699cb118ad5c5470a868b2)
- Change: `dh_ameth: Fix dh_cmp_parameters to really compare the params`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cf1a231d44db81f8565ecae5498a4f1f6f0168c9)
- Fix: `Fix 'openssl speed' information printout`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9e1b6f3cdc9258b6759d00cd23819925c9e4c391)
- Fix X.509 `nc_email` validation of ASN.1 strings containing NUL bytes in the middle. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/485d0790ac1a29a0d4e7391d804810d485890376)
- Change: `MacOS prior to 10.12 does not support random API correctly`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/24cdb1bfecbd765e829b9932a5a60ff63a7dff4b)
- Fix: `Fix the parameter type of gf_serialize`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a7f58bdc1abe245dd09790e8f97d91df271578f4)
- `openssl s_server` adds KTLS option. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2ef7f1265e727567e8963aa2756a387a621ef71)
- `openssl s_client` adds KTLS option. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e396c114eb7233e24ba6a920606cfdd6bc6cff7c)
- Switch `-nameopt` default output to UTF-8. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/86cfd132ffc4f6198cc640a29c293850c0a59914)
- Fix double-free when PRNG seed acquisition fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/52dcc011191ad1a40fd52ae92ef009309deaca52)
- Avoid DRBG error path releasing pointers that were not correctly set. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/caf569a5b3271c2860732ee44509f3825a179fd5)
- Switch to data-driven method for checking TLS RC4 cipher strength. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3b5fa4ab7d19e35311a21fec3ebc0a333c352b6)
- Lower strength rating of CCM_8 cipher suite to reflect its short IV. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a473d1cc67e04ae9fea517b36dc332143250cf5)
- Update documentation: `doc: document the change to the security level of CCM8 cipher suites`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56ffcce492ffc6f36b2f0d9431e23febe054dd04)
- Reduce security strength of CCM8 cipher suite to 64 bits. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e07102220afe4059bc45aa3d7073b7678329e26e)
- Change: `80-test_cmp_http.t: Remove -certout option where not needed`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/75cce8ddee8c108039d0329c4f84466aad0f9c3c)
- Make `OBJ_*` object query calls thread-safe. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/397065c621e733fff80dedb28252120ec143693e)
- Newly allocated NIDs use thread-safe mechanisms where possible. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29c80c6004de8bfd1792e421bbe03ab5f075f21d)
- Add lock protection for OBJ signature-id calls. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c568900c9ac02e92c54bd3168773d54d7350a580)
- Added: `Add sm2 encryption test case from GM/T 0003.5-2012`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ba65c35ea3af347c3b2adc8e665066b541a1c35)
- Fix: `Fix return value of BIO_free`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8f6c533cfcbcad350c9cfb2c112eb9f938ba83c)
- Change: `BIO_ctrl: Avoid spurious error being raised on NULL bio parameter`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/398ae8231650c4bd8ddff0e5efd38233c23b1ca0)
- Allow cipher strings to use standard names. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d1b26ddbf6a9165c71884eff228300e3d83be1b1)
- Added SHA-512 support for RISC-V. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/611685adc04a7c7e9612d51e743044fdcd9d1846)
- Adjusted: `Fully initialise cipher/digest app handles`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a8cc0efe0d8fdd7bfa1d40b3c008d7d6ddf970db)
- AArch64 assembly path supports BTI and pointer authentication. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/19e277dd19f2897f6a7b7eb236abe46655e575bf)
- Fixed `ssl_free()` handling of `BIO_NOCLOSE` to avoid incorrectly closing the associated BIO. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dce910af3bb135bd6d7c5a4cc512043b3ad4acc1)
- Added SHA-256 implementation support for RISC-V. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/657d1927c68bdc3fb0250d16df2a8439e8e043f1)
- Added object identifiers (OIDs) for SM4-GCM/CCM. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a596d38a8cddca4af3416b2664e120028d96e6a9)
- Raised the default TLS security level to 2. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b3a33dac8880b88038083b64d234506659921436)
- Allow sending custom extensions in CertificateRequest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cbb862fbaaa1ec5a3e33836bc92a6dbea97ceba0)
- Prevent integer overflow when caller provides too small a buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/43da9a14f0e73f42f28ae34219929b44df5d1a11)
- Enforce output buffer size check in `EVP_MAC_final()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b97f4dd73b4711eebf731ae0efa6e9b77c7f3304)
- Allow `core_obj_add_sigid` to use an empty digest or no digest specified. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4f716249643fe97a2bdf59a11cc10e1bef8103e9)
- Change: `speed: range check the argument given to -multi`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7220085f22cf6c49933ea8287eb15db57f7ab0db)
- Added: `add checks for the return values of BN_new(), sk_RSA_PRIME_INFO_new_reserve(), EVP_PKEY_CTX_new_from_pkey() and EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new(). Otherwise may result in memory errors.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9dddcd90a1350fa63486cbf3226c3eee79f9aff5)
- Change: `Don't crash encoding a public key with no public key value`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6187d9eac2738e873d23c0c91f9769333b1bb6af)
- Added provider parameter for `ossl_method_construct()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cfce50f791511c8fee7dec90c57f02d9410d039f)
- Added provider-directed generic EVP method fetch internal entry. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2fd3392c8f4e2f3481fa4d7e6a683dc19c6c1cd2)
- Added provider-directed EVP key-management fetch internal entry. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/33561e0d5b89a06d1c03b952196d008b5014914a)
- Adjusted provider fetch order for all PKEY operation paths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5246183e7a9f9fb1819d50ab40e2fecc68235e0d)
- Added internal functions to fetch methods from a specified provider by specific EVP operation type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff7781462dd04ab99c159136b47672252bad7fa8)
- Allow EVP_PKEY operations to fall back to the generic implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/839ffdd11cd48d329a1d89565d62e0be082f9d08)
- Unified check that key is set before operations using EVP_PKEY. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/433e13455ede1a39d415b690b8a564b4f36b8dee)
- Ensure cached algorithm fetch results can be isolated per provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc010ca6ec01d313a84c3c4b040232655a1772ad)
- Added algorithm requirement checks in SM2 signature generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e81c81c9af8a5d22658110d2dc753582eb87a58e)
- Make `DES_set_key()` return value consistent with `DES_set_key_checked()` while still setting the key. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6450ea27ffdc22194f27e90796ce5538af2d81e2)
- Made DES weak key and parity checks constant-time. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8db9d07508e201d95e40f8006ede3a76494bbef3)
- Fixed/prevented: `Fix for a segv interrupt that occurs when fix_dh_rfc5114 is called with ctx->p2 being a null pointer.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09d91264c8ee1fdfcbe41f326a96a21cd85eb732)
- Fixed/prevented: `avoid a NULL dereference when getting digest`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab547fc005307ecf48451638e947cdabca147159)
- Fixed data race when setting `default_DSO_meth`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e6a10b074e90f1ce3d8e9ae0ca740a835ff29bb9)
- Added RPKI `id-ct-ASPA` object identifier. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b0c1214e1e82bc4c98eadd11d368b4ba9ffa202c)
- Fixed handling of AKID/SKID in X.509 certificate extension configuration. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/adbd77f6d7cc4efb7b4bde483036fab8e48ce870)
- Added safe integer overflow helper functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b037e3637a492fefe22b5fb12d7206afe6754ccd)
- Change: `x509: use safe maths calls for overflow detection`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/87fd67d997b236d1202546345d18384a968c9206)
- Avoid writing to global property true/false objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6de9214a5062e9d015c84cbbab681184e16fccaa)
- Only deactivate child provider when needed, avoiding unnecessary unload. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c59fc87b338880893286934f02c446854f5baabf)
- Fixed/prevented: `Avoid a race in init_thread_stop()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b9de0c9aa791bd9e6f0534ec091accbdf15292f)
- Removed/reverted: `Remove the isinited variable from child_prov_globals`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/464c2b988ea149badabaf958a96fdc480df89dc7)
- Change: `Don't try and do ossl_provider_find in ossl_provider_new`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc6d9ede6241e6858f8fa78435d6c8eb9cf85aa1)
- Change: `Don't bail out during provider deactivation if we don't have store`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e39bd6215123f375ddcfe92fa2b2550294da0b73)
- Change: `Stop receiving child callbacks in a child libctx when appropriate`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cad22202a32a94059e351d9819e6c9ed5c66605a)
- Change: `Correctly activate the provider in OSSL_PROVIDER_try_load`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e8ed3e596162d7490b26fb12e58af5208f52402)
- Adjusted: `Use a write lock during ossl_provider_find()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4aced11785f2e54875ad56f30c05bdee02b6e4e2)
- Change: `Hold the flag_lock when calling child callbacks`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/addbd7c9d784e1cb630d43487b0572e867bfc86d)
- Added and used `HAS_PREFIX` / `CHECK_AND_SKIP_PREFIX` prefix check macros. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2ff286c26c29b69b02ca99656d26d2f8cfd54682)
- Added dual 1536/2048-bit exponentiation optimization for Intel Ice Lake. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f87b4c4ea67393c9269663ed40a7ea3463cc59d3)
- `OSSL_PARAM_BLD_push_BN{,_pad}()` now returns an error for negative numbers. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/db65eabefe76e44818ff8bd19c68990e7dcc70d3)
- `openssl x509` returns non-zero exit code when IP/email/host check fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9567fd3819398c6be70508dd6316046da0955b71)
- Allow copying an uninitialized `EVP_MD_CTX`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ece8323ea2230092227bf20e5d93012d15d92e9)
- `OSSL_PARAM_allocate_from_text()` returns an error for unexpected negative input. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8585b5bc62d0bf394ca6adf24f8590e9b9b18402)
- Added sign extension support for `OSSL_PARAM_allocate_from_text()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/946bc0e3ec19ca019fcfa95f93c37f34e12fe0bd)
- Enable Brainpool curves for TLS 1.3. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a10825a009c830125fef94c81d34e41300a24a5)
- Fixed issue where `EVP_PKEY_CTX_get_rsa_pss_saltlen()` did not return a result. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f87463b62f9b2849510d74ff0fd6a62955ea947)
- TLS 1.3 cipher suite list no longer includes disabled suites. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6cb814de6f276106eea39dbb813b9134b1b72041)
- TLS 1.2 GOST cipher no longer uses Encrypt-then-MAC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d724da69389196cdb9ef8db036656882fbc5a6ab)
- Split logic for activating providers from configuration into a separate function. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/07ba69483a7d8005a53284cbde55b9dac8c5c554)
- Fixed diagnostic output and return code when HTTP response line parsing fails. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2b7dc353b353efccd1d228f743baa7c2d2f9f49)
- Fixed parsing when HTTP response status code is not 200. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/38288f424faa0cf61bd705c497bb1a1657611da1)
- CMP application improved diagnostics for missing/extra parameters and unknown ciphers/digests. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9f073575fdb07b486cd1b38974cd177687ccc1e)
- Fixed timeout handling in HTTP redirects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f0d5a3b6ea1bbe4e5dac5b69d853c015db635621)
- Exported `OSSL_provider_init()` as `OPENSSL_EXPORT`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d977a26ed8ca5066d4d72a6d73f1669c8619f4a1)
- Fixed `EVP_PKEY_eq()` comparison support for strict private keys. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3ba62653815b2f7991103cdbea1ac155c8c916a)
- Made CMP's `recipNone` check consistent with transactionID validation logic. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e46997111af3a11632df411b01d62fd39cc3faaf)
- Use `memset` to initialize `bn2binpad` output buffer that will be used later. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/858d5ac16d256db24f78b8c84e723b7d34c8b1ea)
- Fixed carry overflow in MIPS 32-bit `bn_sqr_comba4/8`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/336923c0c8d705cb8af5216b29a205662db0d590)
- Fixed libssl handling of certificate verification errors. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c1c1bb7c5e2baa109baec62d2af09d24caae5557)
- Fixed infinite loop in certificate verification caused by `has_san_id`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6894e20b50c1204bfc990093b4e7ccd10f92865d)
- Added Arm assembly-based RNG support for AArch64. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/efa1f22483ee43d84e1aee01b08c0bda04060c1c)
- Fixed issue where `EC_POINT_hex2point()` did not free point objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dd2fcc1f7c44c5fb5aa2d33aecdc699c7018ce01)
- Fixed use-after-free state in HMAC/MD copy path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad2fcee1632d3f21a37e8e108d4c0dcf9099686d)
- Added: `Add test case to verify that the use after free issue is fixed.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/27f7f527652e403177335eb2e3ba1ff6df13f193)
- HTTP client bypasses issue where SSL BIO does not support `gets` method. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/606c79e29bbc26c27c3b85cc52fe7d72051184de)
- Fixed HTTP client path for cleaning up TLS BIO via `bio_update_fn`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cdaf072f90399efb9e8e19ee4f387d1425f12274)
- Improved efficiency of property index to string conversion using stack structure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e3c59356f847a76a90f9f837d4983428df6eb19)
- Compatible with UI method behavior that always appends NUL terminator. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ef65bbb96352650bf9ce4ff46c60c71d9f138d08)
- Fixed `try_pkcs12()` handling of passphrase NUL terminator. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1dfef929e43ebfa3a7f1108317f75747f92effb6)
- Clear passphrase used by `try_pkcs12()` on the stack. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da7db7ae6d7d1929893a58e41335c88e472fc364)
- Validate category received by `OSSL_trace_end()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee8a61e158c42c327c3303101083422b9a7cc504)
- Fix HTTP client issues caused by the HTTPS proxy callback design. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/068549f8db6d792a88bb888118001c4582f79074)
- Fix double-free of `app_http_tls_cb` when TLS is not used. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/97b8c859c64bc60fcf5bb27ed51489c81fde41b3)
- CMP mock server adds `-ref_cert` option and corresponding API. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b971d4198def0b29654e8fbf7987f7157741aed2)
- New or supported: `trace.c: Add missing trace category entry`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e06c0a2870c55aa4e66108ca071e7da7fd00b922)
- Fix: `fix building failure when using -Wconditional-uninitialized`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/abc4345a19430869b9a8925c6defc9e9ce977429)
- Adjusted: `apps/passwd.c: free before error exiting`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ea4d16bc60dee53feb71997c1e78379eeb69b7ac)
- Fix: `sm2: fix {i2d,d2i}_PublicKey EC_KEY is EVP_PKEY_SM2`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8582dccc4dd1f1667b0e91a098e2cc78c7146dd7)
- Adjusted: `properly free the resource from CRYPTO_malloc`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1b87116a0c43b8b4e1ad88b851d5bcf27c1a5f64)
- Adjusted: `properly free the resource from EVP_MD_CTX_new() at ssl3_record.c:1413`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/949e4f79d202d43519d373b2af6b1a4948bf1a74)
- Fix: `fix the return check of EVP_PKEY_CTX_ctrl() in 5 spots`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7b1264baab7edd82fea8b27d9ddec048bafc0048)
- Fix issue where `EVP_PKEY_fromdata()` still returns a newly allocated key object on failure. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b03b89f7f925384c2768874c95f1af7053fd16f)
- Fix: `OSSL_STORE_open_ex(): Prevent spurious error: unregistered scheme=file`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c64ca71c2ceeb1d47e8499bd351de7d0078ce37)
- Adjusted: `APPS load_key_certs_crls(): Make file access errors much more readable`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e2499474cb96b28a51df1da25cc72f1cf342fad)
- New or supported: `asn1/x_algor.c: add internal ossl_X509_ALGOR_from_nid() simplifying code`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9944df112ffbe4b6855b6a9bf88720803277cc23)
- Adjusted: `v2i_AUTHORITY_KEYID(): Improve error reporting on parsing config values/options`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6e98b7f153fcf9dfad1053fbb3a592166837c6fc)
- Fix: `Fix: some patches related to error exiting`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10481d33844218694929a7bad57314411a33ab74)
- Adjusted: `Update alert to common protocol`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81b741f68984b2620166d0d6271fbd946bab9e7f)
- Adjusted: `Delete unused param about get_construct_message_f`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e1c122711edc3b9d64e506a51c3c0482569b7498)
- Fix: `crypto/bio: fix build on UEFI`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/328bf5adf9e23da523d4195db309083aa02403c4)
- Fix peer key export path in key agreement to match the keymgmt used by the negotiation implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/64a8f6008acce93d0bf184559c63e66c0cc0e23d)
- Adjusted: `Ensure that MDs created via EVP_MD_meth_new() go down the legacy route`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d9ad5b16b32172df6f7d02cfb1c339cc85d0db01)
- Fix: `Fix a leak in EVP_DigestInit_ex()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/357bccc8ba64ec8a5f587b04b5d6b6ca9e8dcbdc)
- New or supported: `Statically link the legacy provider to endecode_test`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7ee992a5d931ab5ad9df00d2d8e47e1b7a72d7ac)
- Refactor or adjust: `PKCS12 app: Improve readability w.r.t. enc_flag, renamed to enc_name`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/870871e5df4f47611c38e81d3f50e38cbf362082)
- Fix: `APPS: Add check for multiple 'unknown' options`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c2724476ef50b8926b033f009bdfc85ac3f1816)
- Fix algorithm fetching and provider resolution for DECODER, ENCODER, and STORE loaders. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd1981a0dc165ab6af5e2945beaaa9efe4484cee)
- Fix: `pkeyutl: Fix regression with -kdflen option`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b82fd89d8bae1445c89ec90d1a6145fe3216d2d7)
- New or supported: `err: add additional errors`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/826da1451b2525b70f93fcc57ed5dbab61a19591)
- New or supported: `params: add error messages for built in param conversions`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac1082f00f991aca1c6e8282717fece16e9bb41f)

- Adjusted: `Enhance code safety and readability in SSL_get_shared_ciphers()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2743594d73e65c38375c619e89ec62579e2c24a9)
- Added passphrase callback support for STORE open extension interface, and set passphrase cache when opening. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96e678087de25c4bb19ef01492bd04002c3fe315)
- Make SSL_net_read_desired() and SSL_net_write_desired() reflect SSL read/write wait states in TLS/DTLS modes respectively. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3432157ba1e0e29bab8bdd31d7ae728930e57c42)
- New or supported: `QUIC APL: Wire SSL_get_[rw]poll_descriptor for TLS/DTLS`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f26244336f2a1b0d3040fe1db5d1024ec40e0b8b)
- New or supported: `BIO: Wire poll descriptor functions in BIO_s_sock`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b79e73cfba2f17cc810bde3c50a2a1d4f03b8cae)
- Adjusted: `cmp_vfy.c: Use verification callback if cert_acceptable() finds expired cert`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/080bd08fd32608b4f2edfa4b1e87e199b08a8835)
- Fix: `Fix error handling in CRYPTO_get_ex_new_index`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d4f22a915ac50570015a23ad794032c4fb9496cb)
- Fix: `Fix error handling in lhash contract`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5fbfd641aeebdf4b29a0749e13a79a1e59502878)
- Fix: `Ensure we free all the BIOs in a chain for QUIC like we do in TLS`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18fd0ea04d6bd37809a4e9a669c49cf9bc146bfb)
- Adjusted: `Do not include sparse_array.o in libssl`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/34d36cdcbdde2e402446994e8384404ca98bfb3f)
- Fix: `no-engine: fix signing with legacy app method based keys`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1acc3e8cc3c69187b55cc557c1bc03278ab38063)
- Adjusted: `Remove a spurious inclusion of the sparse array header file`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/442d08f215c48896f59e9c09a14773058f9e56bf)
- Added public BIO_ADDR_copy() API to copy or clear BIO_ADDR address objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a18c9f80916134bd7122cc1ba204bb5cdca752a3)
- Support parsing five types of X.509v3 NULL-syntax extensions: noRevAvail, sOAIdentifier, noAssertion, groupAC, singleUse, and indirectIssuer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91bc783a93a2a695fe6a2f8da93cf5b5e086ba42)
- Fix: `rand: add extra error code`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a9483b8aa00753a2a9665273c0e376f3c1d36e65)
- Performance optimization: `Optimize out unneeded up_ref/free of EVP_CIPHER`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ed76c62b5d3214e807e684c06efd69c6471c800)
- Fix: `Coverity 1545176: dereference before NULL check`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15410839c668f97b5c03ee1a1bc1a2bf4315715f)
- Adjusted: `Coverity 1545175: use after free`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6bd07949e54f9958eb8a0f9a597ceb3910753ab0)
- New or supported: `obj_xref: ecdsa support sha3 hash function`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/98e0755511fbb5e2563dfe0017f011803d57f51d)
- Adjusted: `obj_xref.h: make update`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de4aa81faaaddcacf0608166406d07bcd641e92b)
- Corrected deprecated version markers for all OPENSSL_LH_stats series functions to 3.1. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a92159d01116495e5e642e55fe0f6e4c821696e)
- Adjusted: `macros.h: There are just 3.1 deprecations, no 3.2 deprecations`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f66602eaa0a034847e9b1347c108b82c60b41d7)
- Adjusted: `Occupy the rec_version field of a decoded dtls record`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b5b2239d8752f8dd413872057c57ef9e1a1a591)
- Adjusted: `Call post_process_record for dtls records`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d3f266446eb5b12a4162804536b7356024977e3)
- Adjusted: `Don't (re-)initialize the FFC_PARAMs in dh_init and dsa_init`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/706512ecbc31585d447b53c3aa89acdf6951f996)
- Adjusted: `Pass the dtls record version to the record layer msg_callback function.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b31597d989f422a0d341be4946d4d64a9251047f)
- Adjusted: `Print record version for DTLSv1_listen()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5f79670f7b9b0354a5f6ccac9474f7a12f86407e)
- Fixed handling of quoted attribute values when converting attribute lists to strings. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fb20e66c6b2651067f50bab8cf098c71e2caed4b)
- Fixed undefined behavior risk of shifting negative values in BN_gcd(). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0f7a3b0caa33a87c900536dc1c02fa553d2193cc)
- Adjusted: `embed bio_dgram_data inside bio_dgram_sctp_data`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4bad474746472f08b0247b5afa81ddc71df98d5f)
- Adjusted: `When calling ossl_crypto_condvar_wait_timeout() we must use real time`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2b8d81534479b161dda063477272363fb2caef08)
- Performance optimization: `* Enable extra Arm64 optimization on Windows for GHASH, RAND and AES`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/636ee1d0b864f29a70573a4894958958e940c01e)
- Fix: `CMP: fix OSSL_CMP_MSG_http_perform() by adding option OSSL_CMP_OPT_USE_TLS`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ac0677bd2394c04632f7ad526879a866b6ed149f)
- Adjusted: `Improve Malloc Failure Test`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3df5736cf303d2c69654ba1c295a9772b738608e)
- Fix: `Ensure struct timeval is defined when including ssl.h`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6cf23ce54d4b9e3b11c8b4807c62ecba27e3aa16)
- Adjusted: `Windows: use srand() instead of srandom()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b107b86ca7d1c6309bc7071ead59acb8c098f3b)
- Fix: `QUIC APL: Fix incoming default stream popping`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd138c33d82cc889fe6a16d18806fbe939279d25)
- Adjusted: `PKCS7_SIGNER_INFO: point out confusing names of digest_enc_alg and enc_digest fields`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e15891b477fe9c3d3dc6f331812c9e8afc48dc05)
- New or supported: `CMS_add1_signer(): add missing ERR_raise() calls`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/72a99ef665b26fa207c0eee6e7e4842d1e42752c)
- Fix: `EVP shake_ctrl(): add missing NULL evp_ctx check`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/410c80dc7bf2085167553ab9fa517189eed2b3a6)
- Fix: `rsa: Accept NULL OAEP label for backward compatibility`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21b98da9d80c561b6273b0c51c259196d6740e70)
- Fix: `fix: LINEAR search doesn't work properly (if CHARSET_EBCDIC is defined)`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a47fc4ed401da4e2d84e035cc4add566e85b03d0)
- Adjusted: `cms encrypt, better OBJ_nid2obj() return check`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bd160912dcc5e39bcdc925d9aa6538f20e37ad16)
- Fix: `ensure that ossl_obj_nid_lock is allocated before use`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cd920f8fa1bb603a620bea697027f5573fadc12e)
- Fix: `ossl_param_build_set_multi_key_bn(): Do not set NULL BIGNUMs`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15a39e7025e0ed4e31664c499894006e41582068)
- Adjusted: `remove sanity check from ossl_rsa_todata`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4ad3a44ba45a4026170336161228d435f6784564)
- Adjusted: `Dont require CRT params on ossl_rsa_set0_all_params`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2647726bd3ca63dc5f07ae3f10e16dff35d95626)
- Adjusted: `Do not include crypto/asn1.h from internal/cryptlib.h`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/80f32964a5388f5a313ced88f17a41f17794e369)
- Adjusted: `cms_enc.c: Include crypto/asn1.h for struct asn1_object_st`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fa9e6ad46860ea92aa2e1ba997b20c6dff76b42c)
- Adjusted: `Ignore retry packets that arrive too late`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56e303259ed48884c914fe24b354e9cc7b7532c3)
- Adjusted: `Ignore ping deadline when calculating tick deadline if we can't send`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/098f27f9ef8be2a418f76896ee3c824e8709fcf7)
- Adjusted: `Postpone two TODO(QUIC) items appropriately`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e520d2714abf4c6254ceec24b57f238433541ee)
- New or supported: `QUIC: Add handling of SSL_get_shutdown()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7757f5ef731ad4e8d6c0f59ef752e4f726ba4f90)
- Fix: `QUIC: Prevent incoming oversize tokens`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/461d41174b33e365677d21bf176d6959b15c2468)
- Adjusted: `Don't encrypt/decrypt packet data during fuzzing`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5415383d2c7e8ee8147eb01361f3f952ceec3761)
- Adjusted: `Don't fail on a bad dcid in the tranport params when fuzzing`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b62ac1abfcac4091cdf8e5e4194c9e3bcc6d382d)
- Adjusted: `Ignore a bad signature in a CertificateVerify message while fuzzing`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/702bb16b9f38c4b17879b0d22bd08ea495c578e2)
- Fix: `Ensure that the ERR_STATE is left in a consistent state`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/94300d8de224e2135e75439e6b9c63eb7ad61fdf)
- Fix: `Fix a use-after-free in qrx_proces_pkt`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1f8a8c1de90ebdb4f3c9dbbf3d1329e3f025e946)
- Fix: `If an error occurs constructing the quic record layer, free it properly`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d8a4451fa76c83ba08b42b38848ba9705fbe71a2)
- Fix: `rand: improve error message for rand pool overflows`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a8faac3c7cc2e88f46a8bdce5bd039dc22abdec)
- Security hardening: process incoming key length and IV length parameters before cipher initialization to avoid these length settings taking effect too late. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3a7e6c057b5054aa05710f3d528b92e3e885268)
- New or supported: `Add notes on use of strdup`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26f75c2d604014069b5ff32cdf7f13f9e6aec5e6)
- Adjusted: `When requeueing deferred URXEs retain the order`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/86e11b1e78ad6937ef32d64ca34013306c9abc28)
- Adjusted: `Make sure we remember how much data we sent in the event of a retry`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9b9f4886f87abd39535721243d4297fd45e558a)
- Added check for SP 800-56B Rev. 2 Section 6.4.1.2.1 Step 3.c, rejecting RSA moduli with non-positive or odd bit counts. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8b268541d9aabee51699aef22963407362830ef9)
- Adjusted: `Make s_client -quic -debug work`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1a91fda1839bc745e8359f82d19a5ef44ca36f7e)
- Extended QUIC connection close information so callers can query the frame type that caused the close. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/55abe7486089ffa24b52e68a56b7eaed9a60a8ee)
- Fix: `Avoid using gets as an argument name in a prototype`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2e471a740b621481b3f3236f82fdd677414900a1)
- Fix: `QUIC TLS: Ensure QUIC_TLS is ticked between each processed RX packet`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/82b7a0eee90e3280bd0e2dd4a9812b3873a7f462)
- Fix: `QUIC CHANNEL: Correct timeout calculation for ACKs`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4d100bb76ad43da75660fa8661d258eaa78fb1c3)
- Adjusted: `QUIC CHANNEL: Tweak crypto buffer sizes`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29f633840df49f29e71a57cc9682d9f3703bfe3b)
- Fix: `QUIC CHANNEL: Handle ping deadlines differently`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c7ed5e4697a71012e0a2d9dd5eaf997754ae5156)
- Fix: `return 0 if an error occurred`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e63c1a1eab6fac262f0d107e058ff02aa1fef065)
- Fixed handling when modular exponentiation result aliases the modulus parameter, and return error for unsupported alias scenarios. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/29c0d8156629a988db5a4af30704736579f7c313)
- Fix: `ssl_lib: added pointer SSL_CONNECTION check to NULL before dereferencing it in ossl_ctrl_internal()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54fa5b3911ead0e1ba7d32bc5732ed2a60b38a99)
- Fix: `ssl_lib: added pointer SSL and SSL_CONNECTION check to NULL before dereferencing it in ossl_ctrl_internal()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6f1d3e130ed9f79335e5c9bd518f835b71417564)
- Fix: `ssl_lib: added pointer SSL and SSL_CONNECTION check to NULL before dereferencing it in ossl_ctrl_internal()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1c03353511238251f48152c09b523322c1de54ad)
- Fix: `ssl_lib: added pointer SSL_CONNECTION check to NULL before dereferencing it in ossl_ctrl_internal()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4230172a0556e1d99dea9469905d18cb64e7c799)
- Fix: `x509_print_ex:Use correct constant for nmflag comparison`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d7f20c79920ba0e74a775bb145bfa9d4a3606492)
- Fixed name format output logic in X509_REQ_print_ex(). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e17656948663adb14b9030aeae70171da5588179)
- Adjusted: `x509_print_ex: Remove unused setting when XN_FLAG_COMPAT is set`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6c6e9d4a18954f9aa4e7e153a4430cc935c18a90)
- Fix: `CMS and PKCS7: fix handlling of EVP_PKEY_get_size() failure`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7eab00ec470693bd44c9de7ab5c06fe691aa3077)
- Fix: `EVP_PKEY_get_{bits,security_bits,size}(): add missing error queue entry on failure`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/51f4115dcc818a35e2c8838c01b3b08740d0c1e1)
- Fix: `free oaep label-octet-string on error`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f77057815be474528ad0e798e08bc9b36a7d4a4d)
- Adjusted: `If the loss detection timer has fired we may not have lost packets`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9d67bd5ffd9514b791917804f7b839a5b3fb6da2)
- Adjusted: `QUIC CHANNEL: Make ping deadline and idle deadline calculation consistent`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e401723baf89b1f201a59fdd679f7ae6b5c123a2)
- Fix: `Check for NULL when freeing the QUIC_TLS object`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e99c771985cbaf6ae55912b581d115f5097fe2fd)
- Added internal API to generate unbiased random integers within a specified range. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3fe56baf936373daa39b944e3194a6f234fbe8bf)
- New or supported: `rand uniform: add comments outlining the algorithm`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7a78528bc540db41ca6834810766a62b640a09c7)
- Fix: `QUIC TXP: Fix drainage calculation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/18a431b6f17204ed2790f5a4fec265ffa5ca061d)
- Performance optimization: `QUIC APL: Optimise write buffer sizes automatically`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96040e539e5ecdf1002e737cb29042f9a065460d)
- New or supported: `Don't create an ack frame if one isn't wanted for this pn_space`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4febab7d808c2c746ded9424ceff4163bdae3278)
- Fix: `Fix a possible memory leak in load_builtin_compressions`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/21f1c2d9d513010f3585d5215b373ac4bef67c29)
- Fix: `QUIC SSTREAM: Fix bug in ossl_quic_sstream_is_totally_acked`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d6b7545b60e72a11894a9fc043325256495473cf)
- Fix: `ossl_quic_new(): Fix a leak found by error injection`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b45d053fcd124854ea5bb7a24bea6a67d31489b0)
- Adjusted: `QUIC CHANNEL: Set reason string for missing tparams extension`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3c7c4866464cfb872b91ba204e3c64d4da9e2fdf)
- New or supported: `QUIC WIRE: Refuse integer transport params with trailing body bytes`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cecc05c2935ef2c93753f126b71103bc6c0c2c7a)
- Fix: `TLS: Fix use of an uninitialized value`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/96e58e32ffd7deaf5184d5e502b476554d39216b)
- Security hardening: added extra input and state checks for HPKE based on code audit. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2a4f8da45c73cff771ae45de46ef73095a6ca29e)
- Fix: `rand uniform: fix likely usage`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/173dca8ea144d9fde41bd129a6a7890fbe3157d1)
- Fix: `ossl_qrl_enc_level_set_provide_secret(): Avoid leaking keyslot in error condition`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/478d14cc3fde378fe722374bc1023c1e0cf00bec)
- Fix: `ossl_quic_new(): Avoid dereferencing NULL qc during cleanup`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e2c6a1d9a0df7a468f73a7ba62a33a860daa9bb6)
- Adjusted: `Correctly track the original length when generating a stream frame`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61a468bfaff43893763f555511300f069ae78746)
- Security hardening: strengthened DH_check_pub_key() and DH_generate_key() against overly large moduli and invalid parameter relationships. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d73028b75b416ccabdc267553dcce241e831eaf3)
- Fix: `Fix a possible memory leak in custom_ext_add`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9f9dc855adf7e9a65b4a0de7fa1ea19d0a55094f)
- Fixed issue in QUIC priority queue where element slots were not reclaimed when deleting the heap top element. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f5f4bc30f303a229d29dea148bc2d289f6fe04e3)
- Fixed handling when implicit-length STREAM frames and PATH_RESPONSE frames coexist in QUIC packet generation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab3b83636e02a12ee678eac8efc1515abe54b687)
- Fixed padding handling for QUIC ACK_ONLY packet type. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/68ed191f6b6541631dd8e298017e83b9b16d5413)
- New or supported: `After a stream has implicit length don't add more stream related frames`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc0dce399aed2c8d2aea8ea675aa3d50f51b6ef4)
- Fix: `Fix a possible memory leak of ssl->s3.tmp.psk`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0fdf6e0a1b16c9f359c63fd94aaa7648dc8ef24c)
- Adjusted: `Force Nonstop to use fcntl(F_GETFL) in BIO_sock_nbio`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ef3e1305db3f77bb0b0702c9b45bb39d165f28a)
- Adjusted QUIC send flow control credit tracking to deduct connection-level consumed credits when adding new stream data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/915ec623eca7b413db6f54ec4aa64585e81ac4df)
- Fixed authentication tag length boundary check in QUIC encrypted packet available space calculation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2aba9548c4a0a3f8359ab5476df3ad58f1cbcf06)
- Zero out structure memory when allocating DTLS handshake fragments to avoid uninitialized fields. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ea038bfa2726ad1bfbc220c8955c0fead9393d5)
- Adjusted: `Move freeing of an old record layer to dtls1_clear_sent_buffer`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2bb83945bb99c98b1a67c5ba6307ad8b0dde5370)
- Fix: `Fix a possible memory leak in dane_tlsa_add`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7f943d40bda4539d63da34ecfbbc8556f2603fb3)
- Fix: `Fix a possible memleak in PKCS7_add_attrib_smimecap`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f06d4082394d4fd6d9ba96a441bbb1c6ef524020)
- Fix: `Fix a possible memleak in CMS_sign_receipt`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/757d6491ebebc541f54c0aa8043b8e5b31a58a7e)
- Fixed potential use-after-free in custom extension release flow, and clear freed array state. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/54e178640dee143742a11842469591dc315d5b5f)
- Fix: `Fix possible memleak in PKCS7_add0_attrib_signing_time`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ad7f4bf979fcb5fb50013b0c9106e26c6ed2fac)

### Provider Plugin Framework
- Adjusted: `VMS: Compensate for x86_64 cross compiler type incompatibility`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9150ca6017f8654e671bd7d7d1b494ce1ce3fa0d)
- Provider encoder adds private key encoder outputting `EncryptedPrivateKeyInfo` format. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0195cdd28fde7d0897e368fdcd4e92509425faad)
- Provider implementation no longer depends on global EVP cipher/digest objects. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e59bfbaa2dbd680f77e1121e382502bd522a466c)
- Default provider adds Keccak-224/256/384/512 digest algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/524f12611040de64cda13dd148ad1b8ca559c730)
- Added provider PVK KDF implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/722fe8edf224ecc0921481b47fdd06a54d82e4ff)
- Default provider adds SM4-GCM implementation (same commit also includes SM4-CCM implementation file). [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c2ee608a234340aa735f894f8d84ead0ce58286e)
- SM2 now returns error when receiving invalid digest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d5d95daba59adc41ab60ea86acd513f255fca3c0)
- Fixed output length handling in SSKDF to avoid claiming buffers smaller than MAC output. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7be8ba546267787c1b0df8a4fddaf9cb29944cbb)
- Allow reinitialization of existing signature/digest-sign operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ae6b68b761b9c5f30897747487ea943ccfab53ba)
- Provider signature algorithms support reinitialization on existing operations. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3ffd23e9529d725903bc97fd45489a77b831876f)
- Default provider adds null digest implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/bef9b48e5071cdd2b41a4f486d1bcb5e14b2a5c3)
- KBKDF supports `R_BITS` of different lengths. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0e9a265e42890699dfce82f1ff6905de6aafbd41)
- Various KDFs always reset `buflen` after clearing output buffer. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d2217c88df6e65c756013417e5ee4f470dd12470)
- Change: `pvkkdf: Always reset buflen after clearing the buffer`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a632bfaa4ee3339749f7a6a07ab4d0abee4eaaef)
- Fixed PVK encoder query for passphrase. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/baa88d9d170b95fd6f177b3e5f8d8818e024a55d)
- PVK decoder now prompts for PVK passphrase instead of PEM passphrase. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/28257d60577932e66934096d0ee8a5dfaca1191e)
- Key to PEM callback now uses passphrase callback passed in parameters. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c22b6592135bfba95a315e438ac7bfc6db461407)
- Updated `OSSL_FUNC_keymgmt_match()` implementations in each provider to match new `EVP_PKEY_eq()` behavior. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ee22a3741e3fc27c981e7f7e9bcb8d3342b0c65a)
- Added RNDRRS provider support. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eb28fda79748c303d88a8af48de5187100f2c64c)
- Fixed/prevented: `Check the return value of ossl_bio_new_from_core_bio()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/352a0bcaab8eda18cce786d2871e8d4ec6f9cbfe)
- Fixed/prevented: `check the return value of EVP_MD_fetch in ecdh_exch.c:285 & dh_exch.c:347`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/04e3ab64d58bb43efc4392d12c607bb4b5a2c562)
- Adjusted: `check the return value of OSSL_PARAM_BLD_new in dsa_kmgmt.c:195`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0da3b39af3d961486758262ca71d2135d7013048)
- Fixed spurious errors when loading private keys via OSSL_STORE. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/da198adb9c5626f31c52613fe2ae59a7066c3366)
- New or supported: `providers/implementations/keymgmt/rsa_kmgmt.c: refactor gen_init`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/22778abad905536fa6c93cdc6fffc8c736dfee79)
- Fix: `Avoid using a macro expansion in a macro when statically initialising`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9c5d1451292566e546d5dd01c7f19950fa34391d)
- New or supported: `drbg: add handling for cases where TSAN isn't available`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8ff861dcee38a41ce93374753e8c462e4b9012e2)
- Adjusted: `Do not call ossl_ffc_name_to_dh_named_group with NULL argument`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b53f88c008d288e86d2bbdc0c4e2d16c29fcee8)
- Adjusted: `replace ;; with ; as statement separator`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d715dbd8e566e7827ce8b2e9b6687c2bcd8a89a0)
- Fixed queryable parameter set for DH key exchange context. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c1167f09d840b109ef1c1c1485e3de64be2fc625)
- Optimize: `SM4 optimization for ARM by HW instruction`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/15b7175f558bf9eb057ec3266685486f727dd70f)
- Fix: `fix indentation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2c9da416a608e2aaf19c16d920baddf2473c8392)
- New or supported: `LEGACY PROV: Reimplement the ERR building blocks in upcall terms`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8c2e588bcf0de61880ec8a956ef57ad6b8a50163)
- Added AES-GCM stitched implementation for POWER9 and newer PPC64LE platforms to improve performance. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44a563dde1584cd9284e80b6e45ee5019be8d36c)
- Optimized AES-GCM: loop unrolling and new instructions for different microarchitectures. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/954f45ba4c504570206ff5bed811e512cf92dc8e)
- Fix: `Fix IV length of DES EDE ECB implementations`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d450eb84c802b2f78971f905b251a0fb89ebb7d1)
- Fix: `Add the missing check of BN_bn2hex return value`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4dd085c03a885580cc945f71187131ea7fb39b70)
- Added safe memory copy helper for provider context duplication. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5b030ec0800d4ad6022ecd00e18a19f77ada0b04)
- New or supported: `pbkdf1: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6585d3aa7638c8cea2d4bb9f10e7298002f652e5)
- New or supported: `pbkdf2: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0a10f71d3071bae0183cd4277da64d100f6b48eb)
- New or supported: `hkdf: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/95bd5ff65985e992827f7178deda84d95b1e6f66)
- New or supported: `kbkdf: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d54c52c28ebb780e2ffc5b7752d35359215cf0a6)
- New or supported: `krb5kdf: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4c1a841c3de645674ed2af92da25f7f5736fae1c)
- New or supported: `pvk kdf: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fe01052f775d1b5dff86ff9b405b6b0df5efd3cf)
- New or supported: `scrypt: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/cdcdcf5c6fa382c879cb3503609519d56fa62e81)
- New or supported: `ssh kdf: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/59558f9d8824747024b6ab756f3798a577ecae48)
- New or supported: `ss KDF: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2722eeceaa993f4488b295a22d2e1178f5ba1ce1)
- New or supported: `k942 kdf: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/769cd46540b2ec2a2d91ee3886b9e4f9d78e9a51)
- New or supported: `pkcs12 kdf: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d3aaf4e9e71944d869ae47821d7b5a8402234ee8)
- New or supported: `tls1 prf: implement ctx dup operation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/b9d8ad3f157fa816c423bec6f7b4328ef894577c)
- Fix: `indentation fix`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1751356267f64d5db8824cf4ff5b3496e15972da)
- Fixed handling of PUBLIC_KEY selection flag in EVP fromdata/todata. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/944f822aadc88b2e25f7695366810c73a53a00c8)
- Fix: `Fix copyrights`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9d987de3aabe54e65a55649a61953966f33b070b)
- Fix: `Fixed counter overflow`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/345c99b6654b8313c792d54f829943068911ddbd)
- Added AVX-512 VAES and VPCLMULQDQ acceleration paths for AES-GCM. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/63b996e752ac698186c38177232280e6515d571b)
- Fix: `dh_exch.c: Add check for OPENSSL_strdup`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c920020f0bb13f0d2bf0fcad5c7ee63458b633b4)
- Fix: `Add define guards to avoid multi-inclusion`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3d27ac8d92ef89c202b518cf6c4e15477eb594b2)
- Further accelerated SM4-GCM on ARM platforms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/26efd0b37714dd1f3557c6b6c32822fc99fe1d9b)
- When using X9.42 KDF, DH derivation always enables padding. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/01b18775676115945956f4de0eb0cafedaf027ab)
- Fixed issue where provider scrypt KDF context copy used wrong construction path. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e04c2c02e8e6b9ec71d93c26c14167ceb2165ce8)
- Refactored `OSSL_LIB_CTX` data management, removing dependency on `CRYPTO_EX_DATA`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/927d0566ded0dff9d6c5abc8a40bb84068446b76)
- Fix: `kdf: avoid NULL dereference on malloc failure in sshkdf`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/148176ca323e3dfce5d5cdb5578c113c8d2440bb)

- Adjusted: `ec_export: Other parameters are exportable with domain parameters`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e20af37d063514c27567c64e975fa5b3208707a9)
- Added SM4 optimized implementation for ARM ASIMD. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4908787f21f4f5fa24b721ed3ebbc4d3e93ef70c)
- Fix: `fix some typos`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ab8b7cd3bef5ae3bcb516a1c2f2fff4abd63c5b)

- New or supported: `Add VERSIONINFO resource to legacy provider if it is not builtin`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9510661400470d357c74c5c4d0ff01c7b813b974)
- Adjusted: `Check return value of ossl_parse_property()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4fa5ed5ce5c345eaeaec8b86eda265add467f941)

- Re-add 3DES to the FIPS provider, but mark it as a non-approved algorithm. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a0ea8ac134e8f503876f19bdc04da69e8862f3a7)
- CMAC parameter setting now returns failure when cipher mode is not CBC. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/94976a1e8d9b127999df14c2e0c38e918c2badda)
- Fix: `Fix the check of EC_GROUP_check_named_curve`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/3b6154ccaf3e64bcdfda4859f2b98ef21b08c5b2)

- EC provider follows `OSSL_PKEY_PARAM_EC_POINT_CONVERSION_FORMAT` settings and uses uncompressed format by default. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a16e86683e8d76c4b9268d757c584b5c971db728)
- Provider adds SM4-XTS implementation. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2788b56f0c8306c89c97a6599484120afddfa14a)

- Implement deterministic ECDSA signatures as per RFC 6979. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f3090fc710e30a749acaf9e5dfbe20dd163cf15d)

- Add option to disable implicit rejection for RSA PKCS#1 v1.5. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/5ab3ec1bb1eaa795d775f5896818cfaa84d33a1a)
- KBKDF adds KMAC support. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/211c47ca1b1ac129dcee59d383cae44e36532bb9)
- Adjusted: `ec_kmgmt.c: check the return of BN_CTX_get() in time.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/467b0492c1e597857b30b91ed72605387aa9825b)
- Adjusted: `Change HKDF to alloc the info buffer.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8115bd1654d5cd7718109679b2047ca573083a8)
- New or supported: `remove extra define for __NR_getrandom and add some comments`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c8a9b26d6ed7a62d26a013c21e62ba4a0a2d6dd1)
- Adjusted: `SSKDF with KMAC should return SIZE_MAX when EVP_KDF_CTX_get_kdf_size() is used.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e8add4d379075a6daef2591edd830297d469b9f4)
- Mark EdDSA as a non-approved algorithm in the FIPS provider. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9fa553247874728cee8ca0ece9aaed476eb0f303)
- Support the five EdDSA instances defined by RFC 8032. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/836080a89a1f5e45dac4e0df76b9270587f65d5b)
- Fix: `s390x: Fix keccak xofs via CPACF`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/76aa4f3ac0d76e58f2111cbf87ae7f25c8766190)
- Fix: `Fix potential NULL pointer dereference`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/235ef96049dbe337a3c3c5d419dacbb5a81df1b3)
- Fix: `S390X: Fix Ed448ph`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f225fbf9521395aff86e85883db9bcb083eab154)
- New or supported: `Allow OSSL_SIGNATURE_PARAM_NONCE_TYPE to be retrieved`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1d857945324810f43a302c9d062c617207093387)
- Fix: `Typos fixing`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91b968bc8e4125a1202e7955961f8e7dfcd17513)
- Re-list X25519 and X448 as FIPS-approved algorithms. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8948b5749410084ed1dfabf17a90df65efcf0f82)
- ChaCha20-Poly1305 no longer accepts truncated IVs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a01152370676e7e11fb461cff8628eb50fa41b81)
- Optimize: `SM4 AESE optimization for ARMv8`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c007203b94b6921ebc8103cb7ae51af554c86afe)
- Prevent creation of DSA/DH keys missing parameters via import. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9ac82e2e7225759c21e712cba6dfe8da22ef7e47)
- Fix: `Do not create DSA keys without parameters by decoder`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/604247bf75571c1c3fb6a1723346c61acd957221)
- Adjusted: `Put EdDSA back as approved algorithms.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/09627a8ceb69e19d2855b36228f44a3660af177a)
- Fix: `des: prevent error when using two key triple DES with a random key`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/587e0407803af330c0b04238fcbce78521ce35d7)
- Adjusted: `Rationalize FIPS sources`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/25624c9087d5422c3bb93cd987a066cb7c883a16)
- Fix: `kbkdf: Fix kbkdf_dup function pointer type`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/344d3b326d573a0eeb5dcbffa643bc06f00023ed)
- Adjusted: `Revert "Put EdDSA back as approved algorithms."`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/759ab5984eb981f2dd165979a7abb950ddad81ae)
- Fix: `Fix incomplete error check on RSA_public_decrypt()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8195e59986031f6f33e2569551d771904433fa04)
- Refactor or adjust: `Remove AES SIV ciphers from the FIPS provider`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c3f985cfd6fb4b8ab1765403d65fed3d006b2163)
- Add accelerated implementation for Keccak XOF on s390x. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/de13699370183ab565f548267afa57e25a921ca9)
- Add FIPS module option to require EMS check for TLS1_PRF KDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/50ea5cdcb735916591e35a04c1f5a659bf253ddc)
- Test changes: `fips: Use salt >= 16 bytes in PBKDF2 selftest`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/451cb23c41c90d5a02902b3a77551aa9ee1c6956)
- Refactor or adjust: `Remove duplicate code`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6b08b786ccba8fb84759a487cca439566a8678c2)
- Fix: `Fix size_t/int mismatch in cms_ec.c and rsa_sig.c`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/559e078d94f1213318105b03f4e88b848fc28314)
- Fix: `Updated `rsa_has()` for correct validation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a3207163ef3d30658a41a9c9e3750ca4c5b16677)
- Refactor or adjust: `Segmentation fault in parent rng cleanup`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6d45fd47f4849c8dc55b8dd5fa1e1b8a158774a0)
- New or supported: `enable VPSM4_EX_CAPABLE for sm4_gcm`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/738d43634a5192b1be0869f151682bb8e9157d5a)
- Test changes: `riscv: Clean up extension test macros`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/86c69fe84118f0dca656d9bfc1131052e2a8e9b8)
- Add XOF support for BLAKE2b provider digest. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/786b9a8d3f8e203c5536e36b9a9bab83bde0311a)
- New or supported: `proverr: add PROV_R_{INVALID_MEMORY_SIZE,INVALID_THREAD_POOL_SIZE}`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/232dd87c55f66ecae906299cbea1ea7782241b64)
- Provider adds Argon2 KDF. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6dfa998f7ea150f9c6d4e4727cf6d5c82a68a8da)
- New or supported: `providers/implementations/kdfs/argon2.c: Include openssl/e_os2.h instead of stdint.h`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46ce0854db51e373ab6ed4982431349107cd9b6d)
- Adjusted: `Coverity 1522032: use after free`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7c45b7cbb04e297c3342fcc50bf7b0a9e36df1dd)
- Fix: `Avoid freeing context on error`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6ec3d3125f76aa9f11c133333f868c42b9b585c4)
- New or supported: `enable hardware acceleration for sm4-ccm`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fdfa63dfd6f1e6d7aff175fba56486a0cf79713f)
- New or supported: `providers/implementations/kdfs/argon2.c: Don't use UINT64_C`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4b738c1ac945a3f1a985df79ff8c37a23d2f2fe0)
- Refactor or adjust: `fips: rework the option handling code`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/83ccf81b1dd8886d54c570354ef8c532af4c514f)
- FIPS provider restricts digest algorithms usable with HMAC DRBG and Hash DRBG. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f553c0f0dd24f037f31d971a99a1ffe7a11f64e6)
- Adjusted: `Declare FIPS option functions in their own header`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/30ab774770a7e8547b0d6363b63a73cc80f33a7b)
- Refactor or adjust: `FFC cleanups`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a76ccb9d0ddc24f6551afbc220b41fb3c4e64c6a)
- Fix: `fix test failure on Kunpeng-920`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/524c2cab6a64f408a5444188c2052b4d76b06775)
- Refactor or adjust: `Minor refactoring of the Argon2 derive function`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/a901b31e99442f087051ae7efdcbc9ad6e6a5b33)
- Adjusted: `Replaced '{ 0, NULL }' with OSSL_DISPATCH_END in OSSL_DISPATCH arrays`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1e6bd31e58dba0bb5d7f21cf1fe1e0d9e4ee3c30)
- Multiple KDF implementations now use shared parameter handling functions. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/345b42be90448523a335b9369452ea1159a1282a)
- EC public key provider parameter reading now returns an error when the output buffer is insufficient. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/91070877adb905f51eb4b19b730d42fc257bae13)
- Adjust HKDF implementation to avoid potentially vulnerable code patterns. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/56a51b5a1ecd54eadc80bed4bfe5044a340787c1)
- Adjusted: `Update the FIPS checksums`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ab17dd8fa3db3e1be82dabfc9fde5dc6181e3f49)
- Fix: `If oaep_md is not initialized, correctly initialize it`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c5aa719502f1ef456b27347e5f7b15c07817da4e)
- New or supported: `Refactor the DRBG implementations to manage locking themselves`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/189ad3ab2028babd39241015fc3975e8334c87eb)
- New or supported: `Enable obtaining certain DRBG params without a lock`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61f11cad7a0dffc7abd234164a0e74c6ae8e7c2a)
- Introduce trie-based fast path for OSSL_PARAM lookup. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e84b5fcc1b1d599173eaab07790c06a532981e50)
- Adjusted: `gcm: use the new faster param location mechanism.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/79d702250b76cb88947158d4f4d0786fbe96eeac)
- Adjusted: `Set RC4 defines on libcrypto/liblegacy`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/58e8af4cecd23dbea2e6b061ab68190b38d64145)
- Adjusted: `Coverity 1531836: Check return value of CRYPTO_atomic_add()`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7efc073dd7ddaed732c35e84efc865463db7ffbc)
- Fix: `Fix build on cygwin`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/9b9c42db3b7e7807e0d3311356fb0316af085171)
- New or supported: `add cygwin CI`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e3b01eb6b25e76d4832d81023d056948edc2cb36)
- Add `no-ecx` configuration option to disable ECX-related features. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4032cd9a1434610e4dc2bbde01f98d04faa615e5)
- Adjusted: `fips: use memory ordering rather than locks`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8e9ca334528e0a923c4deb0af250a60510974be0)
- Fix: `fips: update DSA security check to fix legacy verify strengths`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/71cf587ea21c1422640847e358019a51806d2811)
- New or supported: `fips: use tsan counter instead of tsan_add to increment`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ff934cfdc85a7b8ddb4bdebf9ab68d518bf68b7f)
- Add SHA-256/192 algorithm support. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/81bafac5cbbd195ff9c53a06aaca7c3eacbb2fc0)
- New or supported: `Do not use stitched AES-GCM implementation on PPC32`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/44957a49329135163a4138b1877ccf7f899d19b9)
- Adjusted: `prov(legacy): update to structure based atomics`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7599d17d9385a7fd7489b81dfe560d319931f125)
- New or supported: `Add limited support for WebAssembly WASI target`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d88a0f5f3944535dd83d55bedc4d239544c5678d)
- Fix AES-SIV ignoring empty associated data. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c426c281cfc23ab182f7d7d7a35229e7db1494d9)
- Optimize parsing performance of PKEY decoder. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/32d3c3abf3b74df1d9ebe562ba90f4dc3bdf2d4f)
- Enable ARMv8.2 SHA-3 acceleration on supported Apple CPUs. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/f6484de23df5f04e1f9fa8418e942c45c1a65578)
- Refactor or adjust: `Move CPU detection to armcap.c`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08e6eb216c9d65d502dc136a40e1c0adaefab759)
- Adjust: `Update with `ARMV8_HAVE_SHA3_AND_WORTH_USING``. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ba9472c1c121b13e48f7c198d3fe9871a86e664c)
- New or supported: `Don't add the msblob/pvk decoders if they're not suitable`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6207f2b657b5ba1823681b49c7c34c619da0dd00)
- Fix: `Avoid exporting bogus (empty) data if empty selection is used`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/1ae4678cebaa13604c0f31bdf2c64cd28bdaf287)
- Adjusted: `When exporting/importing decoded keys do not use 0 as selection`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/2acb0d363c0032b5b97c4f6596609f40bd7d842f)
- Fix: `Fix decoders so that they use the passed in propq.`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/39ed7636e0d8a90512e7ccb811cd0bfcb7a79650)
- Implement context duplication for provider ChaCha20 cipher. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/61cfc22b60e33bc77b1e1944759af48c8e58f0d2)
- Adjusted: `make update`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e97c6c3cdf8b4f69e58e8cd610236ae474b9a97f)
- Refactor or adjust: `Remove repeated words`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/ad31628cfef5893b2198077752302a7d9b58135c)
- Provider AEAD ciphers add context duplication support. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0239fb3db77e9de2031c5054854cba8e417c1b72)

- Fix: `Fix aes_gcm_siv dupctx function`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c32c3f2653e6c6ac42e09a83a2f51f8667827a04)
- New or supported: `implement dupctx for chacha20_poly1305`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df93b3c9e72571876bd01e5a50a5ba8368c6c77f)

- Fix: `Fix a memleak in prepare_rsa_params`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46def829afa4d8bed8f53d484bdf842d65f0e176)
- New or supported: `Have legacy blake2 EVP structure use base blake2 implementation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/df9ecd2ef3907ec0a7bf9c54d9273d5342329bf9)
- Fix: `Fix build of SHA3 on ARM64 with no-asm`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/46b43c9f98771139735656e541c8f4c8018c2667)
- Refactor or adjust: `Remove use of _Static_assert`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fc785a554cc37dfa94710b28ced45b03006f0300)
- Fix: `Fix a key repointing in various ciphers`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/0ca5cf989101cae6ffeaef3518e99839fbccb9ba)
- Fix: `Fix new typos found by codespell`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/10fe5e29cad9a9dbaeda3cdc7c62470b21dd7d38)
- Fix: `remove redundant free of NULL`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/c37184f502eb7341e3095ef358a9ebd21facbc46)
- Fix: `fix indentation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6935101354e1ebcb43aa8afb158603f94c0f9bc6)

- Make FIPS random number implementation use the seed source requested by the caller. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/4cde7585ce8eb53682256ba79e6af1949498fbfe)

- Adjusted: `Coverity 1545174: calling risky function`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/eaf08794398ac3caaadffcfd670854bf51f610fa)

- Fix: `Fix coverity alert on use of uninitialised data`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/31fc8a83bc9aa435ae40c3eff713ced441eaa011)

- Fix: `Avoid another copy of key schedule pointer in PROV_GCM_CTX`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/143ca66cf00c88950d689a8aa0c89888052669f4)

- Security hardening: fix handling when random number generator is seeded from a weak entropy source. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/7998e7dc07d8f1f516af32887f2490c03cd8c594)
- Support setting BLAKE2b output size as OSSL_DIGEST_PARAM_SIZE parameter. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6a0ae393dd554eb718e5148696e8f437d4faae5b)

- Security hardening: when changing IV length of AEAD cipher, invalidate previously set IV and require re-setting. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6941eb4d86661488faa3b5c07fc80cfc84ed8934)
- Fix: `update/final: Return error if key is not set`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/e7cb2117c9ccc0d531d521e45ae780be669e4ffc)
- Limit BLAKE2 variable output length to not exceed default digest length. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/aa95fb14003121d0b6c86e564c31cb95424d4bed)
- Adjusted: `Copyright year updates`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8bee92a80174b233a7f4071c57bf14c55c7983aa)

- Adjusted: `always use the same perl in $PATH`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/473664aafdff1f60db99929bdd43c2a9b26d14cd)

- Adjusted: `ci: add copyright header to CI scripts`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/08d8c2d87ec782e95c28ff795e096c2f6f590d63)
- Fix: `Fix util/mkpod2html.pl to call pod2html with absolute paths`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/dc18f036f161e1e49e1d001046716c77d1699e70)
- Adjusted: `Update the default value for the -nameopt option - documentation`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/6923d261b819cdd5d9e0a72337da6d6a92cef2a2)
- Update documentation: `doc: remove end of line whitespace`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/57cd10dd1ee9659b94cfa8a8e74c5a151632975e)
- Optimize: `increase x509 code coverage metrics`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/415e6ac80405e13b20b083315747e431274fbd33)

## Routine Changelog

### New Features
- Added: `Add missing define to enable AES-NI usage on x86 platform`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/d92c696d82b55552da62d6fb71942645315e307a)
- Added: `Add RSA encrypt demo`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/35530b117fcf54cf733c485e9e2e267963c081ee)
- New or supported: `Add the ability to do client side tracing in quictestlib.c`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/8d8c0a901e5d65d68070fbe812d7e8c1449381e1)
- New or supported: `quic_record, quicapi, and quicfaults tests do not support fuzzing builds`. [commit](https://github.com/openssl/openssl/commit/fac61ea4618c83826b51aebf03cbc2bc3ac7b8c8)
